Method: 创建了一个多模态数据集，包括：(1) 在Franka Emika Panda机械臂上通过动觉示教演示从固定起始配置到Jenga塔目标的三种犹豫级别（轻微、显著、极端）运动；(2) 同步RGB-D运动捕捉舞者使用上肢执行相同到达行为的三种犹豫级别，以及极端犹豫下的全身序列

Result: 获得了70条独特的全身轨迹、84条跨越三种犹豫级别的上肢轨迹，以及66条跨越三种犹豫级别的动觉示教轨迹，提供了可重复基准测试的文档

Conclusion: 该数据集为解决犹豫机器人运动设计的挑战提供了重要资源，支持在人机协作中研究犹豫表达对协调策略和安全判断的影响，并促进机器人和人类模态之间的可重复基准测试

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, a robot's expression of hesitancy is a critical factor that shapes human coordination strategies, attention allocation, and safety-related judgments. However, designing hesitant robot motion that generalizes is challenging because the observer's inference is highly dependent on embodiment and context. To address these challenges, we introduce and open-source a multi-modal, dancer-generated dataset of hesitant motion where we focus on specific context-embodiment pairs (i.e., manipulator/human upper-limb approaching a Jenga Tower, and anthropomorphic whole body motion in free space). The dataset includes (i) kinesthetic teaching demonstrations on a Franka Emika Panda reaching from a fixed start configuration to a fixed target (a Jenga tower) with three graded hesitancy levels (slight, significant, extreme) and (ii) synchronized RGB-D motion capture of dancers performing the same reaching behavior using their upper limb across three hesitancy levels, plus full human body sequences for extreme hesitancy. We further provide documentation to enable reproducible benchmarking across robot and human modalities. Across all dancers, we obtained 70 unique whole-body trajectories, 84 upper limb trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels, and 66 kinesthetic teaching trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels. The dataset can be accessed here: https://brsrikrishna.github.io/Dance2Hesitate/.

</details>


### [152] [Characterizing Healthy & Post-Stroke Neuromotor Behavior During 6D Upper-Limb Isometric Gaming: Implications for Design of End-Effector Rehabilitation Robot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10173)
*Ajay Anand,Gabriel Parra,Chad A. Berghoff,Laura A. Hallock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0数据集分析健康和中风后用户在等距轨迹跟踪任务中的力输出、肌肉激活和游戏表现，评估界面设计对行为的影响，分析病理神经运动行为在末端执行器力动力学中的表现，并提出基于HMM的sEMG神经运动行为分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人康复需要设计能促进健康运动实践的游戏和机器人干预，但由于用户神经运动行为、游戏界面和物理机器人之间的复杂相互作用，系统元素设计以及区分"健康"与病理行为具有挑战性。

Method: 利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0开放数据集，分析13名健康用户和2名中风后用户在末端执行器康复机器人上执行等距轨迹跟踪任务时的力输出、肌肉激活和游戏表现。采用任务规范分析、6D末端执行器力数据病理特征检测，以及基于表面肌电信号的隐马尔可夫模型神经运动行为分类方法。

Result: 任务规范（包括约束轴和用户对跟踪指令的解释）影响用户行为；病理相关特征可在等距任务执行期间的6D末端执行器力数据中检测到（健康与中风后用户在力误差和平均力产生方面存在显著差异，p=0.05）；健康神经运动策略具有异质性且难以表征；HMM模型能区分健康和中风后神经运动动力学，而基于协同作用的分解方法无法实现这种区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计能够促进不同用户群体健康运动策略的自适应末端执行器康复机器人提供了重要启示，强调了界面设计对用户行为的影响以及HMM方法在神经运动行为分类中的有效性。

Abstract: Successful robot-mediated rehabilitation requires designing games and robot interventions that promote healthy motor practice. However, the interplay between a given user's neuromotor behavior, the gaming interface, and the physical robot makes designing system elements -- and even characterizing what behaviors are "healthy" or pathological -- challenging. We leverage our OpenRobotRehab 1.0 open access data set to assess the characteristics of 13 healthy and 2 post-stroke users' force output, muscle activations, and game performance while executing isometric trajectory tracking tasks using an end-effector rehabilitation robot. We present an assessment of how subtle aspects of interface design impact user behavior; an analysis of how pathological neuromotor behaviors are reflected in end-effector force dynamics; and a novel hidden Markov model (HMM)-based neuromotor behavior classification method based on surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during cyclic motions. We demonstrate that task specification (including which axes are constrained and how users interpret tracking instructions) shapes user behavior; that pathology-related features are detectable in 6D end-effector force data during isometric task execution (with significant differences between healthy and post-stroke profiles in force error and average force production at $p=0.05$); and that healthy neuromotor strategies are heterogeneous and inherently difficult to characterize. We also show that our HMM-based models discriminate healthy and post-stroke neuromotor dynamics where synergy-based decompositions reflect no such differentiation. Lastly, we discuss these results' implications for the design of adaptive end-effector rehabilitation robots capable of promoting healthier movement strategies across diverse user populations.

</details>


### [153] [Autonomous Search for Sparsely Distributed Visual Phenomena through Environmental Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10174)
*Eric Chen,Travis Manderson,Nare Karapetyan,Peter Edmunds,Nicholas Roy,Yogesh Girdhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用视觉环境上下文（目标物种共生的栖息地特征）来指导AUV在珊瑚礁中高效搜索稀疏分布的目标物种，通过单样本检测和自适应上下文建模，相比仅使用目标检测的策略，能在更短时间内采样更多目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下航行器（AUV）在珊瑚礁调查中面临挑战：目标物种分布稀疏且电池续航有限，无法进行全面搜索。当目标检测本身过于稀疏无法提供方向指导时，需要额外信号来决定下一步搜索方向。

Method: 使用单张标注图像，通过patch级DINOv2嵌入进行在线单样本检测，同时检测目标物种及其周围环境上下文。利用环境上下文特征（比目标检测更密集且变化更平滑）构建奖励函数，使自适应规划器能做出更好的搜索决策。

Result: 在美属维尔京群岛圣约翰两个珊瑚礁站点的真实AUV图像数据上进行离线模拟验证。结果显示：当目标稀疏分布时，该方法能在约一半时间内采样高达75%的目标，优于仅使用目标检测的搜索策略，且显著优于穷举覆盖方法。

Conclusion: 单样本检测结合自适应上下文建模能够实现高效自主调查，利用环境上下文作为额外信号显著提高了AUV在稀疏分布目标搜索中的效率和性能。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are increasingly used to survey coral reefs, yet efficiently locating specific coral species of interest remains difficult: target species are often sparsely distributed across the reef, and an AUV with limited battery life cannot afford to search everywhere. When detections of the target itself are too sparse to provide directional guidance, the robot benefits from an additional signal to decide where to look next. We propose using the visual environmental context -- the habitat features that tend to co-occur with a target species -- as that signal. Because context features are spatially denser and often vary more smoothly than target detections, we hypothesize that a reward function targeted at broader environmental context will enable adaptive planners to make better decisions on where to go next, even in regions where no target has yet been observed. Starting from a single labeled image, our method uses patch-level DINOv2 embeddings to perform one-shot detections of both the target species and its surrounding context online. We validate our approach using real imagery collected by an AUV at two reef sites in St. John, U.S. Virgin Islands, simulating the robot's motion offline. Our results demonstrate that one-shot detection combined with adaptive context modeling enables efficient autonomous surveying, sampling up to 75$\%$ of the target in roughly half the time required by exhaustive coverage when the target is sparsely distributed, and outperforming search strategies that only use target detections.

</details>


### [154] [Octopus-inspired Distributed Control for Soft Robotic Arms: A Graph Neural Network-Based Attention Policy with Environmental Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10198)
*Linxin Hou,Qirui Wu,Zhihang Qin,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftGM：一种受章鱼启发的分布式控制架构，用于分段软体机械臂，通过在线障碍物发现学习在接触丰富的环境中到达目标，无需全局障碍物几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软体机械臂控制方法通常依赖全局环境信息，难以在复杂接触环境中实现鲁棒协调。受章鱼分布式神经控制启发，需要开发能够在未知障碍物环境中自主发现障碍物并协调各段运动的控制架构。

Method: 将每个臂段建模为协作智能体，将臂-环境交互表示为图。采用集中训练分散执行（CTDE）范式，使用两阶段图注意力消息传递方案：集中式评论家和分散式执行器。在Cosserat-rod模拟器（PyElastica）中实现。

Result: 在三种环境复杂度递增的任务中评估：无障碍物、结构化障碍物、带孔墙壁场景。与六种常用MARL基线（IDDPG、IPPO、ISAC、MADDPG、MAPPO、MASAC）相比，SoftGM在简单设置中匹配强CTDE方法，在带孔墙壁任务中表现最佳。在观测噪声、单段驱动故障和瞬态扰动下的鲁棒性测试中，SoftGM保持成功率同时控制努力有界。

Conclusion: SoftGM通过选择性接触相关信息路由实现弹性协调，展示了在接触丰富环境中无需全局障碍物几何信息的有效分布式控制能力。该方法为软体机器人提供了受生物启发的鲁棒协调控制解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes SoftGM, an octopus-inspired distributed control architecture for segmented soft robotic arms that learn to reach targets in contact-rich environments using online obstacle discovery without relying on global obstacle geometry. SoftGM formulates each arm section as a cooperative agent and represents the arm-environment interaction as a graph. SoftGM uses a two-stage graph attention message passing scheme following a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a centralised critic and decentralised actor. We evaluate SoftGM in a Cosserat-rod simulator (PyElastica) across three tasks that increase the complexity of the environment: obstacle-free, structured obstacles, and a wall-with-hole scenario. Compared with six widely used MARL baselines (IDDPG, IPPO, ISAC, MADDPG, MAPPO, MASAC) under identical information content and training conditions, SoftGM matches strong CTDE methods in simpler settings and achieves the best performance in the wall-with-hole task. Robustness tests with observation noise, single-section actuation failure, and transient disturbances show that SoftGM preserves success while keeping control effort bounded, indicating resilient coordination driven by selective contact-relevant information routing.

</details>


### [155] [Perceptive Hierarchical-Task MPC for Sequential Mobile Manipulation in Unstructured Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10227)
*Xintong Du,Jingxing Qian,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种感知层次任务模型预测控制框架，用于在非结构化变化环境中进行高效顺序移动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序移动操作面临独特挑战：机器人长时间操作时，任务成功不仅依赖一致的运动生成，还需要对操作环境变化的感知和适应。现有运动规划器通常假设环境静态且依赖预计算地图，这在长期操作中经常失效，因为半静态变化（如物体移除、引入或移动）很常见。

Method: 提出感知层次任务模型预测控制框架：1) 使用贝叶斯推理框架显式建模物体级变化，维持时间准确的3D环境表示；2) 将最新环境表示嵌入词典优化框架，实现顺序任务的高效执行。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证：相比基线方法，该方法系统性地考虑移动和幻影障碍物，以更高效率和反应性成功完成顺序任务，无需先验地图或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了顺序移动操作中环境变化的挑战，通过显式建模物体级变化和词典优化，实现了在非结构化变化环境中高效、自适应的机器人操作。

Abstract: As compared to typical mobile manipulation tasks, sequential mobile manipulation poses a unique challenge -- as the robot operates over extended periods, successful task completion is not solely dependent on consistent motion generation but also on the robot's awareness and adaptivity to changes in the operating environment. While existing motion planners can generate whole-body trajectories to complete sequential tasks, they typically assume that the environment remains static and rely on precomputed maps. This assumption often breaks down during long-term operations, where semi-static changes such as object removal, introduction, or shifts are common. In this work, we propose a novel perceptive hierarchical-task model predictive control (HTMPC) framework for efficient sequential mobile manipulation in unstructured, changing environments. To tackle the challenge, we leverage a Bayesian inference framework to explicitly model object-level changes and thereby maintain a temporally accurate representation of the 3D environment; this up-to-date representation is embedded in a lexicographic optimization framework to enable efficient execution of sequential tasks. We validate our perceptive HTMPC approach through both simulated and real-robot experiments. In contrast to baseline methods, our approach systematically accounts for moved and phantom obstacles, successfully completing sequential tasks with higher efficiency and reactivity, without relying on prior maps or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [156] [Hierarchical Task Model Predictive Control for Sequential Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10232)
*Xintong Du,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非线性词典优化的分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于移动机械臂的顺序任务执行，相比现有方法在轨迹跟踪性能上提升42%，执行时间快2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，任务规划器能够更好地将人类语言指令转换为任务序列，但需要一种决策算法能够与高层任务规划器无缝对接，高效执行任务序列。

Method: 基于非线性词典优化思想，提出分层任务模型预测控制框架，通过有效利用机器人冗余度来提升顺序任务执行性能。

Result: 相比最先进的任务优先逆运动学控制方法，在任务变化、机器人奇异性和参考变化情况下，分层轨迹跟踪性能平均提升42%；相比典型单任务架构，在任务空间中路径更短，执行时间快2.3倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层任务控制框架能够在9自由度移动机械臂上实现高效顺序任务执行，具有更好的性能和反应性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are envisioned to serve more complex roles in people's everyday lives. With recent breakthroughs in large language models, task planners have become better at translating human verbal instructions into a sequence of tasks. However, there is still a need for a decision-making algorithm that can seamlessly interface with the high-level task planner to carry out the sequence of tasks efficiently. In this work, building on the idea of nonlinear lexicographic optimization, we propose a novel Hierarchical-Task Model Predictive Control framework that is able to complete sequential tasks with improved performance and reactivity by effectively leveraging the robot's redundancy. Compared to the state-of-the-art task-prioritized inverse kinematic control method, our approach has improved hierarchical trajectory tracking performance by 42% on average when facing task changes, robot singularity and reference variations. Compared to a typical single-task architecture, our proposed hierarchical task control architecture enables the robot to traverse a shorter path in task space and achieves an execution time 2.3 times faster when executing a sequence of delivery tasks. We demonstrated the results with real-world experiments on a 9 degrees of freedom mobile manipulator.

</details>


### [157] [Degeneracy-Resilient Teach and Repeat for Geometrically Challenging Environments Using FMCW Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10248)
*Katya M. Papais,Wenda Zhao,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于FMCW激光雷达的退化环境鲁棒性Teach and Repeat导航系统，通过多普勒测速里程计和退化感知扫描-地图定位解决传统ICP方法在几何退化环境中的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ICP的T&R系统在几何退化环境（如平坦、稀疏结构地形）中容易变得病态，导致定位性能下降和导航不可靠，需要一种能在退化环境中稳定工作的导航方案。

Method: 1) 利用FMCW激光雷达提供的多普勒径向速度测量，开发几何无关、无对应关系的运动估计方法，包含稳定的姿态不确定性估计；2) 提出退化感知定位方法，结合点云曲率改进数据关联，统一平移和旋转尺度以实现一致的退化检测。

Result: 在三种不同结构丰富度的环境中进行闭环现场实验，包括具有挑战性的平坦机场测试场，所提系统能可靠完成自主导航，而传统ICP系统在该环境下失败。

Conclusion: 基于FMCW激光雷达的退化弹性T&R导航系统能够有效解决几何退化环境中的定位问题，为GPS拒止环境下的可靠自主导航提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Teach and Repeat (T&R) topometric navigation enables robots to autonomously repeat previously traversed paths without relying on GPS, making it well suited for operations in GPS-denied environments such as underground mines and lunar navigation. State-of-the-art T&R systems typically rely on iterative closest point (ICP)-based estimation; however, in geometrically degenerate environments with sparsely structured terrain, ICP often becomes ill-conditioned, resulting in degraded localization and unreliable navigation performance. To address this challenge, we present a degeneracy-resilient Frequency-Modulated Continuous-Wave (FMCW) lidar T&R navigation system consisting of Doppler velocity-based odometry and degeneracy-aware scan-to-map localization. Leveraging FMCW lidar, which provides per-point radial velocity measurements via the Doppler effect, we extend a geometry-independent, correspondence-free motion estimation to include principled pose uncertainty estimation that remains stable in degenerate environments. We further propose a degeneracy-aware localization method that incorporates per-point curvature for improved data association, and unifies translational and rotational scales to enable consistent degeneracy detection. Closed-loop field experiments across three environments with varying structural richness demonstrate that the proposed system reliably completes autonomous navigation, including in a challenging flat airport test field where a conventional ICP-based system fails.

</details>


### [158] [From Prior to Pro: Efficient Skill Mastery via Distribution Contractive RL Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10263)
*Zhanyi Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DICE-RL是一个强化学习框架，通过"分布收缩"操作将预训练的生成式机器人策略精炼为高性能策略，利用在线反馈放大高成功率行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练的生成式机器人策略虽然具有广泛的行为覆盖，但性能可能不够出色。需要一种方法能够将这种广泛的行为先验转化为高性能策略，同时保持稳定性和样本效率。

Method: 首先预训练基于扩散或流的策略以获得广泛行为覆盖，然后通过稳定、样本高效的残差离策略RL框架进行微调。该框架结合了选择性行为正则化和价值引导的动作选择。

Result: DICE-RL在实验中可靠地提升了性能，表现出强大的稳定性和样本效率。能够直接从高维像素输入中掌握复杂的长时程操作技能，在仿真和真实机器人上均验证有效。

Conclusion: DICE-RL提供了一种有效的框架，能够将预训练的生成式行为先验转化为高性能策略，为复杂机器人技能学习提供了稳定且样本高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Distribution Contractive Reinforcement Learning (DICE-RL), a framework that uses reinforcement learning (RL) as a "distribution contraction" operator to refine pretrained generative robot policies. DICE-RL turns a pretrained behavior prior into a high-performing "pro" policy by amplifying high-success behaviors from online feedback. We pretrain a diffusion- or flow-based policy for broad behavioral coverage, then finetune it with a stable, sample-efficient residual off-policy RL framework that combines selective behavior regularization with value-guided action selection. Extensive experiments and analyses show that DICE-RL reliably improves performance with strong stability and sample efficiency. It enables mastery of complex long-horizon manipulation skills directly from high-dimensional pixel inputs, both in simulation and on a real robot. Project website: https://zhanyisun.github.io/dice.rl.2026/.

</details>


### [159] [Design of a Robot-Assisted Chemical Dialysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10264)
*Diane Jung,Caleb Escobedo,Noah Liska,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Daniel Szafir,Alessandro Roncone,Carson Bruns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种用于透析实验的机器人辅助系统，通过用户中心设计方法优化，旨在减轻科学家在长时间实验中的工作负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验中的手动操作繁琐耗时，成为现代实验科学的瓶颈，特别是在材料科学和医学领域。透析作为聚合物和蛋白质合成中常用的多日纯化方法，需要自动化解决方案来解放科学家

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法，通过两次可用性研究收集参与者反馈，修订设计需求，开发出满足科学家需求的最终系统

Result: 开发出满足科学家需求的机器人辅助透析系统，该系统有潜力应用于其他实验工作流程，并能有效减轻科学家在长时间实验中的工作负担

Conclusion: 该系统集成到化学湿实验室的实际合成程序中，将减少科学家在长时间实验中的工作量，为设计更多加速科学发现、解放科学家繁琐劳动的系统提供了有效方法

Abstract: Scientists perform diverse manual procedures that are tedious and laborious. Such procedures are considered a bottleneck for modern experimental science, as they consume time and increase burdens in fields including material science and medicine. We employ a user-centered approach to designing a robot-assisted system for dialysis, a common multi-day purification method used in polymer and protein synthesis. Through two usability studies, we obtain participant feedback and revise design requirements to develop the final system that satisfies scientists' needs and has the potential for applications in other experimental workflows. We anticipate that integration of this system into real synthesis procedures in a chemical wet lab will decrease workload on scientists during long experimental procedures and provide an effective approach to designing more systems that have the potential to accelerate scientific discovery and liberate scientists from tedious labor.

</details>


### [160] [Update-Free On-Policy Steering via Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10282)
*Maria Attarian,Ian Vyse,Claas Voelcker,Jasper Gerigk,Evgenii Opryshko,Anas Almasri,Sumeet Singh,Yilun Du,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UF-OPS：一种无需更新的在线策略引导方法，通过训练验证器函数来预测动作成功概率，引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作，提升行为克隆策略的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）方法虽然流行，但策略往往脆弱，在精确操作任务中表现不佳。需要一种能够在不修改基础策略参数的情况下提升策略鲁棒性的轻量级方法

Method: 提出UF-OPS（Update-Free On-Policy Steering）方法：1）在策略初始评估阶段收集策略rollout数据；2）使用这些数据训练验证器函数来预测动作的成功概率；3）在执行时使用验证器引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作；4）保持基础策略参数不变，实现轻量级和灵活性

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法对黑盒扩散策略平均提升了49%的成功率，覆盖5个真实任务，且无需修改基础策略参数

Conclusion: UF-OPS是一种有效的在线策略引导方法，能够显著提升行为克隆策略的鲁棒性和成功率，同时保持轻量级和灵活性，适用于实际机器人应用

Abstract: In recent years, Behavior Cloning (BC) has become one of the most prevalent methods for enabling robots to mimic human demonstrations. However, despite their successes, BC policies are often brittle and struggle with precise manipulation. To overcome these issues, we propose UF-OPS, an Update-Free On-Policy Steering method that enables the robot to predict the success likelihood of its actions and adapt its strategy at execution time. We accomplish this by training verifier functions using policy rollout data obtained during an initial evaluation of the policy. These verifiers are subsequently used to steer the base policy toward actions with a higher likelihood of success. Our method improves the performance of black-box diffusion policy, without changing the base parameters, making it light-weight and flexible. We present results from both simulation and real-world data and achieve an average 49% improvement in success rate over the base policy across 5 real tasks.

</details>


### [161] [SteadyTray: Learning Object Balancing Tasks in Humanoid Tray Transport via Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10306)
*Anlun Huang,Zhenyu Wu,Soofiyan Atar,Yuheng Zhi,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReST-RL：一种分层强化学习架构，通过解耦步态与负载稳定，实现双足机器人动态搬运中的托盘稳定控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在非结构化环境中进行动态行走时，未固定负载会因步态振荡而产生不稳定，这是当前人形机器人应用的关键工程瓶颈

Method: 提出ReST-RL分层强化学习架构：1）底层为鲁棒的基础步态策略；2）上层为动态残差模块，专门设计用于主动抵消末端执行器处的步态诱导扰动。这种架构分离避免了端到端学习的局限性

Result: 在仿真中，残差设计在步态平滑度和方向精度上显著优于端到端基线，实现96.9%的变速度跟踪成功率和74.5%的外部力扰动鲁棒性。成功部署于Unitree G1人形机器人硬件，展示了优秀的零样本仿真到现实泛化能力

Conclusion: ReST-RL的模块化方法通过解耦步态与负载稳定，实现了双足机器人动态搬运中的可靠稳定控制，为复杂环境下的机器人应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Stabilizing unsecured payloads against the inherent oscillations of dynamic bipedal locomotion remains a critical engineering bottleneck for humanoids in unstructured environments. To solve this, we introduce ReST-RL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning architecture that explicitly decouples locomotion from payload stabilization, evaluated via the SteadyTray benchmark. Rather than relying on monolithic end-to-end learning, our framework integrates a robust base locomotion policy with a dynamic residual module engineered to actively cancel gait-induced perturbations at the end-effector. This architectural separation ensures steady tray transport without degrading the underlying bipedal stability. In simulation, the residual design significantly outperforms end-to-end baselines in gait smoothness and orientation accuracy, achieving a 96.9% success rate in variable velocity tracking and 74.5% robustness against external force disturbances. Successfully deployed on the Unitree G1 humanoid hardware, this modular approach demonstrates highly reliable zero-shot sim-to-real generalization across various objects and external force disturbances.

</details>


### [162] [PC-Diffuser: Path-Consistent Capsule CBF Safety Filtering for Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10330)
*Eugene Ku,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PC-Diffuser：一种将可证明的安全屏障函数结构嵌入扩散规划去噪循环的安全增强框架，实现自动驾驶轨迹生成中的迭代安全保障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂交通中的自动驾驶需要超越手工规则的数据驱动规划器，但基于扩散的轨迹规划器虽然闭环性能强，却难以认证且在罕见或分布外场景可能发生灾难性故障，需要解决安全认证问题

Method: 提出PC-Diffuser框架，将可证明的路径一致屏障函数结构直接嵌入扩散规划的去噪循环中：1)使用胶囊距离屏障函数评估碰撞风险，更好反映车辆几何并减少不必要的保守性；2)将去噪航点转换为运动学自行车模型下的动态可行运动；3)应用路径一致安全滤波器消除残余约束违规而不产生几何失真

Result: 通过在每个去噪步骤注入安全一致校正并将精炼轨迹反馈回扩散过程，PC-Diffuser实现了迭代、上下文感知的安全保障，而非后处理修复，使安全成为轨迹生成的内在部分

Conclusion: PC-Diffuser通过将可证明的安全结构嵌入扩散规划循环，解决了数据驱动轨迹规划器的安全认证问题，实现了安全与学习性能的更好平衡

Abstract: Autonomous driving in complex traffic requires planners that generalize beyond hand-crafted rules, motivating data-driven approaches that learn behavior from expert demonstrations. Diffusion-based trajectory planners have recently shown strong closed-loop performance by iteratively denoising a full-horizon plan, but they remain difficult to certify and can fail catastrophically in rare or out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this challenge, we present PC-Diffuser, a safety augmentation framework that embeds a certifiable, path-consistent barrier-function structure directly into the denoising loop of diffusion planning. The key idea is to make safety an intrinsic part of trajectory generation rather than a post-hoc fix: we enforce forward invariance along the rollout while preserving the diffusion model's intended path geometry. Specifically, PC-Diffuser (i) evaluates collision risk using a capsule-distance barrier function that better reflects vehicle geometry and reduces unnecessary conservativeness, (ii) converts denoised waypoints into dynamically feasible motion under a kinematic bicycle model, and (iii) applies a path-consistent safety filter that eliminates residual constraint violations without geometric distortion, so the corrected plan remains close to the learned distribution. By injecting these safety-consistent corrections at every denoising step and feeding the refined trajectory back into the diffusion process, PC-Diffuser enables iterative, context-aware safeguarding instead of post-hoc repair...

</details>


### [163] [Adaptive Manipulation Potential and Haptic Estimation for Tool-Mediated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10352)
*Lin Yang,Anirvan Dutta,Yuan Ji,Yanxin Zhou,Shilin Shan,Lv Chen,Etienne Burdet,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出参数化平衡流形作为工具交互的统一表示，开发集成触觉估计、在线规划和自适应刚度控制的闭环框架，通过触觉SLAM和不确定性感知阻抗控制实现鲁棒的工具操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的工具操作中，视觉遮挡和触觉感知的不确定性是实现人类水平灵巧性的主要挑战，需要新的表示和控制方法来处理这些复杂交互。

Method: 1) 引入参数化平衡流形作为工具交互的统一表示；2) 建立物理-几何对偶性，使用包含可微接触模型的自适应操作势能；3) 将触觉估计重新表述为流形参数估计问题，采用混合推理策略（触觉SLAM）；4) 通过连续更新操作势能参数动态重塑平衡流形，指导在线轨迹重规划和实现不确定性感知阻抗控制。

Result: 在仿真和260多次真实世界螺丝松动试验中验证了系统性能。实验结果表明：在标准场景中实现了鲁棒的识别和操作成功率，同时保持准确跟踪；消融研究证实触觉SLAM和不确定性感知刚度调制优于固定阻抗基线，有效防止紧密公差交互中的卡滞。

Conclusion: 参数化平衡流形为工具介导的交互提供了统一的表示框架，通过触觉SLAM和自适应刚度控制的闭环集成，实现了对视觉遮挡和触觉不确定性的鲁棒处理，为复杂接触丰富的工具操作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in contact-rich, tool-mediated manipulation remains a significant challenge due to visual occlusion and the underdetermined nature of haptic sensing. This paper introduces a parameterized Equilibrium Manifold (EM) as a unified representation for tool-mediated interaction, and develops a closed-loop framework that integrates haptic estimation, online planning, and adaptive stiffness control. We establish a physical-geometric duality using an adaptive manipulation potential incorporating a differentiable contact model, which induces the manifold's geometric structure and ensures that complex physical interactions are encapsulated as continuous operations on the EM. Within this framework, we reformulate haptic estimation as a manifold parameter estimation problem. Specifically, a hybrid inference strategy (haptic SLAM) is employed in which discrete object shapes are classified via particle filtering, while the continuous object pose is estimated using analytical gradients for efficient optimization. By continuously updating the parameters of the manipulation potential, the framework dynamically reshapes the induced EM to guide online trajectory replanning and implement uncertainty-aware impedance control, thereby closing the perception-action loop. The system is validated through simulation and over 260 real-world screw-loosening trials. Experimental results demonstrate robust identification and manipulation success in standard scenarios while maintaining accurate tracking. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that haptic SLAM and uncertainty-aware stiffness modulation outperform fixed impedance baselines, effectively preventing jamming during tight tolerance interactions.

</details>


### [164] [Few-Shot Adaptation to Non-Stationary Environments via Latent Trend Embedding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10373)
*Yasuyuki Fujii,Emika Kameda,Hiroki Fukada,Yoshiki Mori,Tadashi Matsuo,Nobutaka Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在趋势ID的少样本适应框架，通过估计低维环境状态而非修改模型参数来应对非平稳环境中的概念漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在真实环境中常面临概念漂移问题，即输入-输出关系因潜在环境因素变化而改变。传统适应方法通过更新模型参数会导致灾难性遗忘和高计算成本，需要更高效的适应方案。

Method: 提出潜在趋势ID框架：1) 通过反向传播估计低维环境状态（趋势ID），保持模型参数固定；2) 引入时间正则化防止过拟合；3) 使用状态转移模型强制潜在空间平滑演化。

Result: 在定量食物抓取任务实验中，学习的趋势ID在潜在空间中分布在不同的区域并具有时间一致的轨迹，能够在未修改模型参数的情况下实现少样本适应到未见环境。

Conclusion: 该框架为在多样化和演化环境中运行的机器人应用提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案，通过估计环境状态而非修改模型权重来实现高效适应。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in real-world environments often suffer from concept shift, where the input-output relationship changes due to latent environmental factors that are not directly observable. Conventional adaptation methods update model parameters, which may cause catastrophic forgetting and incur high computational cost. This paper proposes a latent Trend ID-based framework for few-shot adaptation in non-stationary environments. Instead of modifying model weights, a low-dimensional environmental state, referred to as the Trend ID, is estimated via backpropagation while the model parameters remain fixed. To prevent overfitting caused by per-sample latent variables, we introduce temporal regularization and a state transition model that enforces smooth evolution of the latent space. Experiments on a quantitative food grasping task demonstrate that the learned Trend IDs are distributed across distinct regions of the latent space with temporally consistent trajectories, and that few-shot adaptation to unseen environments is achieved without modifying model parameters. The proposed framework provides a scalable and interpretable solution for robotics applications operating across diverse and evolving environments.

</details>


### [165] [ScanDP: Generalizable 3D Scanning with Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10390)
*Itsuki Hirako,Ryo Hakoda,Yubin Liu,Matthew Hwang,Yoshihiro Sato,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的3D扫描框架，使用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略，通过占据栅格映射和混合空间表示方法，在未见物体上实现高覆盖率和短路径扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的3D扫描方法需要大量训练数据，且难以泛化到未见物体类别。需要开发数据高效且泛化能力强的扫描框架。

Method: 采用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略；使用占据栅格映射替代直接点云处理以增强鲁棒性；结合球体空间表示和路径优化程序确保路径安全性和扫描效率。

Result: 在形状和尺度各异的未见物体上评估，相比基线方法获得更高覆盖率和更短路径，对传感器噪声保持鲁棒性，在真实世界执行中验证了实用可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的数据高效3D扫描框架通过模仿人类策略和鲁棒的空间表示方法，成功解决了传统方法的泛化问题，在未见物体上实现了高效准确的扫描。

Abstract: Learning-based 3D Scanning plays a crucial role in enabling efficient and accurate scanning of target objects. However, recent reinforcement learning-based methods often require large-scale training data and still struggle to generalize to unseen object categories.In this work, we propose a data-efficient 3D scanning framework that uses Diffusion Policy to imitate human-like scanning strategies. To enhance robustness and generalization, we adopt the Occupancy Grid Mapping instead of direct point cloud processing, offering improved noise resilience and handling of diverse object geometries. We also introduce a hybrid approach combining a sphere-based space representation with a path optimization procedure that ensures path safety and scanning efficiency. This approach addresses limitations in conventional imitation learning, such as redundant or unpredictable behavior. We evaluate our method on diverse unseen objects in both shape and scale. Ours achieves higher coverage and shorter paths than baselines, while remaining robust to sensor noise. We further confirm practical feasibility and stable operation in real-world execution.

</details>


### [166] [Safe Probabilistic Planning for Human-Robot Interaction using Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10392)
*Jake Gonzales,Kazuki Mizuta,Karen Leung,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制的新型概率安全控制框架，用于人机交互场景，通过量化CBF安全值的预测误差并提供形式化安全保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，复杂的人类行为给机器人安全控制带来挑战，需要既能提供形式化安全保证又能适应人类行为不确定性的控制方法

Method: 将控制屏障函数与保形风险控制相结合，使用保形风险控制来量化和控制CBF安全值的预测误差，建立约束满足概率的形式化保证，并引入基于当前交互上下文动态调整安全边界的算法

Result: 在人机导航场景实验中，相比基线方法显著降低了碰撞率和安全违规，同时在目标到达任务中保持了高成功率和高效率控制

Conclusion: 该框架为人机交互提供了具有形式化安全保证的概率安全控制方法，在保证安全性的同时保持了任务性能，代码和仿真材料已公开

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel probabilistic safe control framework for human-robot interaction that combines control barrier functions (CBFs) with conformal risk control to provide formal safety guarantees while considering complex human behavior. The approach uses conformal risk control to quantify and control the prediction errors in CBF safety values and establishes formal guarantees on the probability of constraint satisfaction during interaction. We introduce an algorithm that dynamically adjusts the safety margins produced by conformal risk control based on the current interaction context. Through experiments on human-robot navigation scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces collision rates and safety violations as compared to baseline methods while maintaining high success rates in goal-reaching tasks and efficient control. The code, simulations, and other supplementary material can be found on the project website: https://jakeagonzales.github.io/crc-cbf-website/.

</details>


### [167] [Rethinking Gaussian Trajectory Predictors: Calibrated Uncertainty for Safe Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10407)
*Fatemeh Cheraghi Pouria,Mahsa Golchoubian,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于校准轨迹预测不确定性的新损失函数，通过核密度估计匹配经验分布与卡方分布，提高高斯轨迹预测器置信水平的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯轨迹预测器主要依赖负对数似然损失，容易产生过度自信或不足自信的分布，影响下游不确定性感知规划器的安全性。预测置信水平的可靠性问题未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出一种新颖的损失函数，利用核密度估计计算置信水平的经验分布，通过显式匹配经验分布与卡方分布来强制符合高斯假设特性。同时结合均方误差项确保均值预测准确性。

Result: 在真实世界轨迹数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提高了不同最先进高斯轨迹预测器的置信水平可靠性。与不确定性感知模型预测控制集成后，通过校准的置信水平在复杂场景中实现了改进的规划性能。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了高斯轨迹预测器置信水平校准问题，为不确定性感知规划器提供了可靠的概率洞察，在复杂场景中实现了更好的碰撞避免导航性能。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction is critical for safe autonomous navigation in crowded environments. While many trajectory predictors output Gaussian distributions to represent the multi-modal distribution over future pedestrian positions, the reliability of their confidence levels often remains unaddressed. This limitation can lead to unsafe or overly conservative motion planning when the predictor is integrated with an uncertainty-aware planner. Existing Gaussian trajectory predictors primarily rely on the Negative Log-Likelihood loss, which is prone to predict over- or under-confident distributions, and may compromise downstream planner safety. This paper introduces a novel loss function for calibrating prediction uncertainty which leverages Kernel Density Estimation to estimate the empirical distribution of confidence levels. The proposed formulation enforces consistency with the properties of a Gaussian assumption by explicitly matching the estimated empirical distribution to the Chi-squared distribution. To ensure accurate mean prediction, a Mean Squared Error term is also incorporated in the final loss formulation. Experimental results on real-world trajectory datasets show that our method significantly improves the reliability of confidence levels predicted by different State-Of-The-Art Gaussian trajectory predictors. We also demonstrate the importance of providing planners with reliable probabilistic insights (i.e. calibrated confidence levels) for collision-free navigation in complex scenarios. For this purpose, we integrate Gaussian trajectory predictors trained with our loss function with an uncertainty-aware Model Predictive Control on scenarios extracted from real-world datasets, achieving improved planning performance through calibrated confidence levels.

</details>


### [168] [AsyncMDE: Real-Time Monocular Depth Estimation via Asynchronous Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10438)
*Lianjie Ma,Yuquan Li,Bingzheng Jiang,Ziming Zhong,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsyncMDE：一种异步深度感知系统，通过基础模型和轻量级模型的异步协作，在边缘设备上实现高效的单目深度估计，显著降低计算成本同时保持较高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的单目深度估计计算成本高，难以部署在边缘平台。现有方法对每帧进行独立推理，浪费了连续机器人操作中相邻视角间的计算冗余。

Method: 系统由基础模型和轻量级模型组成：基础模型在后台生成高质量空间特征；轻量级模型在前台异步运行，通过互补融合将缓存记忆与当前观测结合，输出深度估计并自回归更新记忆，实现跨帧特征重用。

Result: 仅3.83M参数，在RTX 4090上达到237 FPS，恢复基础模型77%的精度差距，参数减少25倍。在室内静态、动态和合成极端运动基准测试中验证，在Jetson AGX Orin上通过TensorRT实现161FPS。

Conclusion: AsyncMDE通过异步架构和特征重用机制，在边缘设备上实现了高效实时的深度感知，为机器人实时部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation-model-based monocular depth estimation offers a viable alternative to active sensors for robot perception, yet its computational cost often prohibits deployment on edge platforms. Existing methods perform independent per-frame inference, wasting the substantial computational redundancy between adjacent viewpoints in continuous robot operation. This paper presents AsyncMDE, an asynchronous depth perception system consisting of a foundation model and a lightweight model that amortizes the foundation model's computational cost over time. The foundation model produces high-quality spatial features in the background, while the lightweight model runs asynchronously in the foreground, fusing cached memory with current observations through complementary fusion, outputting depth estimates, and autoregressively updating the memory. This enables cross-frame feature reuse with bounded accuracy degradation. At a mere 3.83M parameters, it operates at 237 FPS on an RTX 4090, recovering 77% of the accuracy gap to the foundation model while achieving a 25X parameter reduction. Validated across indoor static, dynamic, and synthetic extreme-motion benchmarks, AsyncMDE degrades gracefully between refreshes and achieves 161FPS on a Jetson AGX Orin with TensorRT, clearly demonstrating its feasibility for real-time edge deployment.

</details>


### [169] [KnowDiffuser: A Knowledge-Guided Diffusion Planner with LM Reasoning and Prior-Informed Trajectory Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10441)
*Fan Ding,Xuewen Luo,Fengze Yang,Bo Yu,HwaHui Tew,Ganesh Krishnasamy,Junn Yong Loo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KnowDiffuser是一个知识引导的运动规划框架，通过结合语言模型的语义理解能力和扩散模型的轨迹生成能力，解决自动驾驶中语义到物理的鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型具有强大的语义推理能力但无法生成连续物理轨迹，扩散模型能生成可靠轨迹但缺乏语义可解释性和场景级理解，需要结合两者优势解决自动驾驶规划问题。

Method: 使用语言模型从结构化场景表示中推断上下文感知的元动作，映射到先验轨迹锚定去噪过程；采用两阶段截断去噪机制高效细化轨迹，保持语义对齐和物理可行性。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，KnowDiffuser在开环和闭环评估中显著优于现有规划器，建立了鲁棒且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: KnowDiffuser有效弥合了自动驾驶系统中的语义到物理鸿沟，将语言模型的语义理解与扩散模型的生成能力紧密结合，为自动驾驶规划提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Language Models (LMs) have demonstrated strong semantic reasoning capabilities, enabling their application in high-level decision-making for autonomous driving (AD). However, LMs operate over discrete token spaces and lack the ability to generate continuous, physically feasible trajectories required for motion planning. Meanwhile, diffusion models have proven effective at generating reliable and dynamically consistent trajectories, but often lack semantic interpretability and alignment with scene-level understanding. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{KnowDiffuser}, a knowledge-guided motion planning framework that tightly integrates the semantic understanding of language models with the generative power of diffusion models. The framework employs a language model to infer context-aware meta-actions from structured scene representations, which are then mapped to prior trajectories that anchor the subsequent denoising process. A two-stage truncated denoising mechanism refines these trajectories efficiently, preserving both semantic alignment and physical feasibility. Experiments on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that KnowDiffuser significantly outperforms existing planners in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, establishing a robust and interpretable framework that effectively bridges the semantic-to-physical gap in AD systems.

</details>


### [170] [FAR-Dex: Few-shot Data Augmentation and Adaptive Residual Policy Refinement for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10451)
*Yushan Bai,Fulin Chen,Hongzheng Sun,Yuchuang Tong,En Li,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAR-Dex是一个分层框架，通过少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼实现机器人臂手协调的灵巧操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手与机械臂协作实现类人灵巧操作面临高质量演示稀缺和高维动作空间复杂性的挑战

Method: 提出FAR-Dex分层框架：1) FAR-DexGen利用IsaacLab模拟器从少量演示生成多样化物理约束轨迹；2) FAR-DexRes引入自适应残差模块，结合多步轨迹段和观测特征精炼策略

Result: 实验表明FAR-Dex将数据质量提升13.4%，任务成功率提高7%优于现有方法，在真实世界任务中达到80%以上成功率，具有强位置泛化能力

Conclusion: FAR-Dex通过少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼有效解决了臂手协调灵巧操作的数据稀缺和策略优化问题，实现了精确鲁棒的精细操作

Abstract: Achieving human-like dexterous manipulation through the collaboration of multi-fingered hands with robotic arms remains a longstanding challenge in robotics, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations and the complexity of high-dimensional action spaces. To address these challenges, we propose FAR-Dex, a hierarchical framework that integrates few-shot data augmentation with adaptive residual refinement to enable robust and precise arm-hand coordination in dexterous tasks. First, FAR-DexGen leverages the IsaacLab simulator to generate diverse and physically constrained trajectories from a few demonstrations, providing a data foundation for policy training. Second, FAR-DexRes introduces an adaptive residual module that refines policies by combining multi-step trajectory segments with observation features, thereby enhancing accuracy and robustness in manipulation scenarios. Experiments in both simulation and real-world demonstrate that FAR-Dex improves data quality by 13.4% and task success rates by 7% over state-of-the-art methods. It further achieves over 80% success in real-world tasks, enabling fine-grained dexterous manipulation with strong positional generalization.

</details>


### [171] [BinWalker: Development and Field Evaluation of a Quadruped Manipulator Platform for Sustainable Litter Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10529)
*Giulio Turrisi,Angelo Bratta,Giovanni Minelli,Gabriel Fischer Abati,Amir H. Rad,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于户外自主垃圾收集的四足机器人系统，结合了腿式移动的优势与机械臂操作能力，旨在解决传统人工清理在复杂地形中的效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾污染是全球性环境问题，特别是在不平坦地形、海岸环境等难以进入的区域。传统人工清理劳动强度大、成本高、效率低，需要自动化解决方案来支持大规模清理操作。

Method: 开发了一个四足机器人系统，结合腿式移动平台（提供复杂地形导航能力）和机械臂操作系统（配备车载垃圾容器）。系统集成了感知、移动和操作功能，能够自主检测、抓取和存储垃圾物品。

Result: 在户外场景的实验评估表明，该系统能够有效执行垃圾收集任务，证明了腿式机器人平台在环境清理任务中集成感知、移动和操作的可行性，特别是在传统机器人平台难以到达的环境中。

Conclusion: 该四足机器人系统展示了在复杂户外环境中自主垃圾收集的潜力，为大规模垃圾清理操作提供了有前景的解决方案，特别是在难以进入的区域，有助于减轻人工清理的负担。

Abstract: Litter pollution represents a growing environmental problem affecting natural and urban ecosystems worldwide. Waste discarded in public spaces often accumulates in areas that are difficult to access, such as uneven terrains, coastal environments, parks, and roadside vegetation. Over time, these materials degrade and release harmful substances, including toxic chemicals and microplastics, which can contaminate soil and water and pose serious threats to wildlife and human health. Despite increasing awareness of the problem, litter collection is still largely performed manually by human operators, making large-scale cleanup operations labor-intensive, time-consuming, and costly. Robotic solutions have the potential to support and partially automate environmental cleanup tasks. In this work, we present a quadruped robotic system designed for autonomous litter collection in challenging outdoor scenarios. The robot combines the mobility advantages of legged locomotion with a manipulation system consisting of a robotic arm and an onboard litter container. This configuration enables the robot to detect, grasp, and store litter items while navigating through uneven terrains. The proposed system aims to demonstrate the feasibility of integrating perception, locomotion, and manipulation on a legged robotic platform for environmental cleanup tasks. Experimental evaluations conducted in outdoor scenarios highlight the effectiveness of the approach and its potential for assisting large-scale litter removal operations in environments that are difficult to reach with traditional robotic platforms. The code associated with this work can be found at: https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/trash-collection-isaaclab.

</details>


### [172] [TacLoc: Global Tactile Localization on Objects from a Registration Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10565)
*Zirui Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Fumin Zhang,Huan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacLoc：一种新颖的触觉定位框架，将触觉姿态估计建模为一次性点云配准任务，无需渲染数据或预训练模型，通过图论部分到完整配准方法实现高效准确的位置估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人抓取过程中，视觉感知常因夹爪与物体交互而被遮挡，现有触觉方法依赖触觉仿真或预训练模型，限制了泛化能力和效率。

Method: 提出TacLoc框架，将触觉定位问题建模为一次性点云配准任务，采用图论部分到完整配准方法，利用触觉感知的密集点云和表面法线，通过法线引导的图剪枝和假设验证流程实现姿态估计。

Result: 在YCB数据集上进行了广泛评估，并在两种不同的视觉-触觉传感器上对真实世界物体进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: TacLoc无需渲染数据或预训练模型，通过图论部分到完整配准方法实现了高效准确的触觉姿态估计，在机器人操作中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Pose estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, particularly when visual perception is occluded during gripper-object interactions. Existing tactile-based methods generally rely on tactile simulation or pre-trained models, which limits their generalizability and efficiency. In this study, we propose TacLoc, a novel tactile localization framework that formulates the problem as a one-shot point cloud registration task. TacLoc introduces a graph-theoretic partial-to-full registration method, leveraging dense point clouds and surface normals from tactile sensing for efficient and accurate pose estimation. Without requiring rendered data or pre-trained models, TacLoc achieves improved performance through normal-guided graph pruning and a hypothesis-and-verification pipeline. TacLoc is evaluated extensively on the YCB dataset. We further demonstrate TacLoc on real-world objects across two different visual-tactile sensors.

</details>


### [173] [Safety-critical Control Under Partial Observability: Reach-Avoid POMDP meets Belief Space Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10572)
*Matti Vahs,Joris Verhagen,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层证书控制架构，将POMDP中的目标到达、信息收集和安全行为解耦，使用BCLF和BCBF实现实时控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线POMDP求解器将目标到达、安全和主动信息收集三个行为统一在单一信念树搜索中，但这种方法难以处理这些目标之间固有的冲突时间尺度问题

Method: 提出分层证书控制架构，在信念空间中解耦三个行为：1) 使用信念控制李雅普诺夫函数(BCLF)形式化信息收集问题；2) 使用信念控制屏障函数(BCBF)结合保形预测提供有限时域概率安全保证；3) 控制综合简化为轻量级二次规划，可实时求解

Result: 在仿真和空间机器人平台上验证了实时性能，相比最先进的约束POMDP求解器，提高了安全性和任务成功率，即使对于维度>10^4的非高斯信念表示也能实时求解

Conclusion: 分层证书控制架构有效解决了POMDP中目标冲突的时间尺度问题，通过解耦和模块化设计实现了实时控制，为机器人不确定环境下的决策提供了新框架

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a principled framework for robot decision-making under uncertainty. Solving reach-avoid POMDPs, however, requires coordinating three distinct behaviors: goal reaching, safety, and active information gathering to reduce uncertainty. Existing online POMDP solvers attempt to address all three within a single belief tree search, but this unified approach struggles with the conflicting time scales inherent to these objectives. We propose a layered, certificate-based control architecture that operates directly in belief space, decoupling goal reaching, information gathering, and safety into modular components. We introduce Belief Control Lyapunov Functions (BCLFs) that formalize information gathering as a Lyapunov convergence problem in belief space, and show how they can be learned via reinforcement learning. For safety, we develop Belief Control Barrier Functions (BCBFs) that leverage conformal prediction to provide probabilistic safety guarantees over finite horizons. The resulting control synthesis reduces to lightweight quadratic programs solvable in real time, even for non-Gaussian belief representations with dimension $>10^4$. Experiments in simulation and on a space-robotics platform demonstrate real-time performance and improved safety and task success compared to state-of-the-art constrained POMDP solvers.

</details>


### [174] [Recover to Predict: Progressive Retrospective Learning for Variable-Length Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10597)
*Hao Zhou,Lu Qi,Jason Li,Jie Zhang,Yi Liu,Xu Yang,Mingyu Fan,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出渐进式回顾框架（PRF）解决自动驾驶轨迹预测中可变长度不完整观测的问题，通过级联回顾单元逐步对齐特征，并采用滚动启动训练策略提高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法通常在固定长度观测下优化预测精度，但现实驾驶场景往往产生可变长度、不完整观测。直接将不完整观测特征映射到完整观测特征的一次性映射方法难以准确学习短轨迹表示，因为存在显著信息差距。

Method: 提出渐进式回顾框架（PRF），通过级联回顾单元逐步将不完整观测特征与完整观测特征对齐。每个单元包含回顾蒸馏模块（RDM）和回顾预测模块（RPM）：RDM蒸馏特征，RPM使用蒸馏特征恢复先前时间步。还提出滚动启动训练策略（RSTS）提高PRF训练时的数据效率。PRF可与现有方法即插即用。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Argoverse 1数据集上进行广泛实验，证明了PRF的有效性。

Conclusion: PRF通过渐进式特征对齐机制有效解决了可变长度不完整观测下的轨迹预测问题，提高了预测准确性，且具有良好的兼容性和数据效率。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is critical for autonomous driving, enabling safe and efficient planning in dense, dynamic traffic. Most existing methods optimize prediction accuracy under fixed-length observations. However, real-world driving often yields variable-length, incomplete observations, posing a challenge to these methods. A common strategy is to directly map features from incomplete observations to those from complete ones. This one-shot mapping, however, struggles to learn accurate representations for short trajectories due to significant information gaps. To address this issue, we propose a Progressive Retrospective Framework (PRF), which gradually aligns features from incomplete observations with those from complete ones via a cascade of retrospective units. Each unit consists of a Retrospective Distillation Module (RDM) and a Retrospective Prediction Module (RPM), where RDM distills features and RPM recovers previous timesteps using the distilled features. Moreover, we propose a Rolling-Start Training Strategy (RSTS) that enhances data efficiency during PRF training. PRF is plug-and-play with existing methods. Extensive experiments on datasets Argoverse 2 and Argoverse 1 demonstrate the effectiveness of PRF. Code is available at https://github.com/zhouhao94/PRF.

</details>


### [175] [AdaClearGrasp: Learning Adaptive Clearing for Zero-Shot Robust Dexterous Grasping in Densely Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10616)
*Zixuan Chen,Wenquan Zhang,Jing Fang,Ruiming Zeng,Zhixuan Xu,Yiwen Hou,Xinke Wang,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaClearGrasp：一种用于密集杂乱环境中自适应清理和零样本灵巧抓取的闭环决策执行框架，通过视觉语言模型进行高层决策，结合强化学习策略实现跨物体泛化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集杂乱环境中，物理干扰、视觉遮挡和不稳定接触常导致直接灵巧抓取失败，而激进的分离策略可能危及安全。机器人需要自适应地决定是直接抓取目标还是先清理周围物体，以实现鲁棒操作。

Method: 提出闭环决策执行框架AdaClearGrasp，将操作建模为可控高层决策过程。使用预训练视觉语言模型解释视觉观察和语言任务描述，推理抓取干扰并生成高层规划骨架，通过统一动作接口调用结构化原子技能。为灵巧抓取训练强化学习策略，采用相对手-物体距离表示，实现跨物体几何和物理属性的零样本泛化。执行中利用视觉反馈监控结果并在失败时触发重规划。

Result: 引入Clutter-Bench作为首个具有分级杂乱复杂度的仿真基准，包含7个目标物体和3个杂乱级别，共210个任务场景。在3个物体和3个杂乱级别（18个场景）上进行仿真到真实实验。结果表明AdaClearGrasp在密集杂乱环境中显著提高了抓取成功率。

Conclusion: AdaClearGrasp通过结合高层决策、视觉语言推理和零样本灵巧抓取，有效解决了密集杂乱环境中的操作挑战，实现了自适应清理与抓取的闭环框架，显著提升了复杂场景下的操作鲁棒性。

Abstract: In densely cluttered environments, physical interference, visual occlusions, and unstable contacts often cause direct dexterous grasping to fail, while aggressive singulation strategies may compromise safety. Enabling robots to adaptively decide whether to clear surrounding objects or directly grasp the target is therefore crucial for robust manipulation. We propose AdaClearGrasp, a closed-loop decision-execution framework for adaptive clearing and zero-shot dexterous grasping in densely cluttered environments. The framework formulates manipulation as a controllable high-level decision process that determines whether to directly grasp the target or first clear surrounding objects. A pretrained vision-language model (VLM) interprets visual observations and language task descriptions to reason about grasp interference and generate a high-level planning skeleton, which invokes structured atomic skills through a unified action interface. For dexterous grasping, we train a reinforcement learning policy with a relative hand-object distance representation, enabling zero-shot generalization across diverse object geometries and physical properties. During execution, visual feedback monitors outcomes and triggers replanning upon failures, forming a closed-loop correction mechanism. To evaluate language-conditioned dexterous grasping in clutter, we introduce Clutter-Bench, the first simulation benchmark with graded clutter complexity. It includes seven target objects across three clutter levels, yielding 210 task scenarios. We further perform sim-to-real experiments on three objects under three clutter levels (18 scenarios). Results demonstrate that AdaClearGrasp significantly improves grasp success rates in densely cluttered environments. For more videos and code, please visit our project website: https://chenzixuan99.github.io/adaclear-grasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [176] [Interleaving Scheduling and Motion Planning with Incremental Learning of Symbolic Space-Time Motion Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10651)
*Elisa Tosello,Arthur Bit-Monnot,Davide Lusuardi,Alessandro Valentini,Andrea Micheli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合调度与运动规划的增量学习框架，用于解决共享工作空间中的多物体导航问题，通过调度器与运动规划器的交互迭代生成可行计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化仓库等现实场景中，任务通常是预定义的，主要挑战在于如何在资源、时间和运动约束下安全高效地决定是否、何时以及如何执行这些任务。传统任务与运动规划方法需要同时处理高层任务序列和低层运动规划，而在任务预定义场景中，问题转变为调度与运动规划的集成问题。

Method: 提出一个新颖的解决方案框架，将现成的调度器和运动规划器在增量学习循环中交织使用。调度器生成候选计划，运动规划器检查可行性并返回符号反馈（空间冲突和时间调整），引导调度器向运动可行的解决方案演进。

Result: 在增强了运动任务的物流和作业车间调度基准上验证了该框架，使用了最先进的调度器和基于采样的运动规划器。结果表明该框架在复杂时空约束下能有效生成有效计划，特别是在同步运动至关重要的场景中。

Conclusion: 该研究将调度与运动规划问题形式化为共享工作空间中的多物体导航问题，提出的增量学习框架能够有效集成调度器和运动规划器，在复杂时空约束下生成可行的执行计划，为自动化仓库等实际应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Task and Motion Planning combines high-level task sequencing (what to do) with low-level motion planning (how to do it) to generate feasible, collision-free execution plans. However, in many real-world domains, such as automated warehouses, tasks are predefined, shifting the challenge to if, when, and how to execute them safely and efficiently under resource, time and motion constraints. In this paper, we formalize this as the Scheduling and Motion Planning problem for multi-object navigation in shared workspaces. We propose a novel solution framework that interleaves off-the-shelf schedulers and motion planners in an incremental learning loop. The scheduler generates candidate plans, while the motion planner checks feasibility and returns symbolic feedback, i.e., spatial conflicts and timing adjustments, to guide the scheduler towards motion-feasible solutions. We validate our proposal on logistics and job-shop scheduling benchmarks augmented with motion tasks, using state-of-the-art schedulers and sampling-based motion planners. Our results show the effectiveness of our framework in generating valid plans under complex temporal and spatial constraints, where synchronized motion is critical.

</details>


### [177] [STM32-Based Smart Waste Bin for Hygienic Disposal Using Embedded Sensing and Automated Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10660)
*Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan,Aritra Islam Saswato,Md. Misbah Khan,Anish Paul,Ahmed Faizul Haque Dhrubo,Mohammad Abdul Qayum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于STM32微控制器、超声波传感器和伺服电机的运动感应自动垃圾桶设计与实现，提供非接触式垃圾处理方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共场所和私人环境对卫生和非接触式解决方案的需求日益增长，推动了日常应用的自动化系统开发

Method: 使用STM32微控制器控制超声波传感器检测用户存在，通过伺服电机自动打开垃圾桶盖；额外超声波传感器监测内部垃圾量，OLED显示屏提供实时系统状态反馈

Result: 实验评估显示系统具有快速响应时间、稳定的传感性能和流畅的机械操作，可有效部署于家庭、教育机构、医院和公共设施

Conclusion: 该系统提供了一种低成本、可靠且易于部署的非接触式垃圾处理解决方案，能够改善卫生状况和用户便利性

Abstract: The increasing demand for hygienic and contactless solutions in public and private environments has encouraged the development of automated systems for everyday applications. This paper presents the design and implementation of a motion- sensing automatic waste bin using an STM32 microcontroller, ultrasonic sensors, and a servo motor. The system detects user presence through ultrasonic sensing and automatically opens the bin lid using a servo motor controlled by the microcontroller. An additional ultrasonic sensor is used to monitor the internal waste level of the bin, while an OLED display provides real- time feedback regarding system status. The proposed system offers a low-cost, reliable, and easily deployable solution for touch-free waste disposal. Experimental evaluation demonstrates fast response time, stable sensing performance, and smooth mechanical operation. The system can be effectively deployed in homes, educational institutions, hospitals, and public facilities to improve hygiene and user convenience.

</details>


### [178] [Dynamic Modeling and Attitude Control of a Reaction-Wheel-Based Low-Gravity Bipedal Hopper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10670)
*Shriram Hari,M Venkata Sai Nikhil,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球等低重力环境的欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，采用内部反作用飞轮在弹道飞行阶段调节身体姿态，通过动量交换控制器减少空中姿态偏差，实现稳定着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球和近地小行星等低重力天体由于接触力减小和空中停留时间延长，对移动系统提出特殊挑战。跳跃运动虽然能量效率高，但容易因不对称推力产生和地形相互作用导致空中姿态不稳定。需要开发能够在弹道飞行阶段主动控制姿态的解决方案。

Method: 设计欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，采用内部反作用飞轮调节身体姿态。将系统建模为陀螺体，分析躯干旋转与反作用飞轮动量之间的动态耦合。运动周期分为三个阶段：腿驱动推进跳跃、通过主动动量交换控制器进行空中姿态稳定、减震着陆。开发降阶模型捕捉躯干旋转与反作用飞轮动力学的关键耦合。在MuJoCo仿真环境中基于月球重力条件(g=1.625 m/s²)进行评估。

Result: 激活反作用飞轮控制器可将峰值空中角偏差减少65%以上，并将着陆姿态误差限制在3.5度以内。同时减少了每个跳跃周期的执行器饱和，确保足够的控制能力。该方法显著减轻了飞行中的姿态偏移，实现了稳定直立着陆。

Conclusion: 提出的欠驱动双足跳跃机器人框架通过内部反作用飞轮和主动动量交换控制器，有效解决了低重力环境中跳跃运动的姿态不稳定问题。该方法为不规则外星地形上的移动提供了实用且控制效率高的解决方案，能够实现一致的直立着陆，提高了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Planetary bodies characterized by low gravitational acceleration, such as the Moon and near-Earth asteroids, impose unique locomotion constraints due to diminished contact forces and extended airborne intervals. Among traversal strategies, hopping locomotion offers high energy efficiency but is prone to mid-flight attitude instability caused by asymmetric thrust generation and uneven terrain interactions. This paper presents an underactuated bipedal hopping robot that employs an internal reaction wheel to regulate body posture during the ballistic flight phase. The system is modeled as a gyrostat, enabling analysis of the dynamic coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel momentum. The locomotion cycle comprises three phases: a leg-driven propulsive jump, mid-air attitude stabilization via an active momentum exchange controller, and a shock-absorbing landing. A reduced-order model is developed to capture the critical coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel dynamics. The proposed framework is evaluated in MuJoCo-based simulations under lunar gravity conditions (g = 1.625 m/s^2). Results demonstrate that activation of the reaction wheel controller reduces peak mid-air angular deviation by more than 65% and constrains landing attitude error to within 3.5 degrees at touchdown. Additionally, actuator saturation per hop cycle is reduced, ensuring sufficient control authority. Overall, the approach significantly mitigates in-flight attitude excursions and enables consistent upright landings, providing a practical and control-efficient solution for locomotion on irregular extraterrestrial terrains.

</details>


### [179] [OnFly: Onboard Zero-Shot Aerial Vision-Language Navigation toward Safety and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10682)
*Guiyong Zheng,Yueting Ban,Mingjie Zhang,Juepeng Zheng,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OnFly是一个完全机载的实时零样本空中视觉语言导航框架，通过共享感知双智能体架构、混合关键帧-近期帧记忆、语义-几何验证器和后退视野规划器，解决了现有方法决策不稳定、长视野进度监测不可靠以及安全与效率权衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本空中视觉语言导航方法存在三个主要问题：1）单流视觉语言模型决策不稳定；2）长视野进度监测不可靠；3）安全与效率之间的权衡。这些问题限制了无人机在复杂3D环境中执行自然语言指令的能力。

Method: OnFly采用共享感知双智能体架构，将高频目标生成与低频进度监测解耦。使用混合关键帧-近期帧记忆保持全局轨迹上下文和KV缓存前缀稳定性。引入语义-几何验证器利用VLM特征和深度线索优化目标预测。采用后退视野规划器在几何安全约束下生成优化轨迹。

Result: 在仿真实验中，OnFly将任务成功率从26.4%提升至67.8%，相比最强基线有显著改进。完全机载的真实世界飞行验证了其实时部署可行性。

Conclusion: OnFly通过创新的架构设计解决了零样本AVLN中的关键挑战，实现了稳定决策、可靠长视野监测以及安全与效率的平衡，为完全机载实时视觉语言导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (AVLN) enables UAVs to follow natural-language instructions in complex 3D environments. However, existing zero-shot AVLN methods often suffer from unstable single-stream Vision-Language Model decision-making, unreliable long-horizon progress monitoring, and a trade-off between safety and efficiency. We propose OnFly, a fully onboard, real-time framework for zero-shot AVLN. OnFly adopts a shared-perception dual-agent architecture that decouples high-frequency target generation from low-frequency progress monitoring, thereby stabilizing decision-making. It further employs a hybrid keyframe-recent-frame memory to preserve global trajectory context while maintaining KV-cache prefix stability, enabling reliable long-horizon monitoring with termination and recovery signals. In addition, a semantic-geometric verifier refines VLM-predicted targets for instruction consistency and geometric safety using VLM features and depth cues, while a receding-horizon planner generates optimized collision-free trajectories under geometric safety constraints, improving both safety and efficiency. In simulation, OnFly improves task success from 26.4% to 67.8%, compared with the strongest state-of-the-art baseline, while fully onboard real-world flights validate its feasibility for real-time deployment. The code will be released at https://github.com/Robotics-STAR-Lab/OnFly

</details>


### [180] [MapGCLR: Geospatial Contrastive Learning of Representations for Online Vectorized HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10688)
*Jonas Merkert,Alexander Blumberg,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过引入地理空间一致性对比损失和重叠轨迹数据集划分方法，提出了一种半监督的在线高精地图构建方法，在减少标注需求的同时提升了向量化地图感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线高精地图创建和维护成本高昂，在线地图构建需要大量标注数据。为了减少标注需求，需要探索自监督学习方法，通过利用未标注数据提升模型性能。

Method: 1. 在向量化在线高精地图构建模型中，通过对比损失函数强制重叠鸟瞰图特征网格之间的地理空间一致性；2. 提出分析数据集内轨迹重叠的方法，生成符合多轨迹要求的子数据集划分；3. 采用半监督方法：在少量单轨迹标注数据上进行监督训练，在大量多轨迹未标注数据上进行自监督训练。

Result: 该方法在各方面都优于监督基线：1. 在向量化地图感知性能的定量评估中表现更好；2. 在鸟瞰图特征空间的主成分分析可视化中，分割质量在定性评估中更优。

Conclusion: 通过地理空间一致性对比损失和多轨迹数据划分策略，提出的半监督方法能够有效利用未标注数据，在减少标注需求的同时显著提升在线高精地图构建的性能。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely on map information to understand the world around them. However, the creation and maintenance of offline high-definition (HD) maps remains costly. A more scalable alternative lies in online HD map construction, which only requires map annotations at training time. To further reduce the need for annotating vast training labels, self-supervised training provides an alternative. This work focuses on improving the latent birds-eye-view (BEV) feature grid representation within a vectorized online HD map construction model by enforcing geospatial consistency between overlapping BEV feature grids as part of a contrastive loss function. To ensure geospatial overlap for contrastive pairs, we introduce an approach to analyze the overlap between traversals within a given dataset and generate subsidiary dataset splits following adjustable multi-traversal requirements. We train the same model supervised using a reduced set of single-traversal labeled data and self-supervised on a broader unlabeled set of data following our multi-traversal requirements, effectively implementing a semi-supervised approach. Our approach outperforms the supervised baseline across the board, both quantitatively in terms of the downstream tasks vectorized map perception performance and qualitatively in terms of segmentation in the principal component analysis (PCA) visualization of the BEV feature space.

</details>


### [181] [Parallel-in-Time Nonlinear Optimal Control via GPU-native Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10711)
*Yilin Zou,Zhong Zhang,Fanghua Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出完全GPU原生的轨迹优化框架，结合序列凸规划与基于共识的ADMM，通过时间分裂策略实现大规模并行计算，在边缘计算平台上实现4倍吞吐量提升和51%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时轨迹优化方法主要依赖CPU顺序求解器，受限于全局稀疏线性代数或动态规划算法的串行特性，无法充分利用GPU等大规模并行计算架构，需要开发完全GPU原生的优化框架。

Method: 结合序列凸规划与基于共识的交替方向乘子法，采用时间分裂策略将优化时域解耦为独立的节点子问题，实现完全GPU原生并行计算，避免昂贵的内存传输和大规模稀疏分解。

Result: 在四旋翼敏捷飞行和火星动力下降任务中验证，相比12核CPU基准实现持续4倍吞吐量加速和51%能耗降低，GPU利用率超过96%，规划频率超过100Hz，并可扩展至鲁棒模型预测控制。

Conclusion: 该完全GPU原生轨迹优化框架通过大规模并行计算显著提升性能效率，为自主系统提供可扩展的实时规划能力，支持鲁棒MPC应用，实现安全自主性。

Abstract: Real-time trajectory optimization for nonlinear constrained autonomous systems is critical and typically performed by CPU-based sequential solvers. Specifically, reliance on global sparse linear algebra or the serial nature of dynamic programming algorithms restricts the utilization of massively parallel computing architectures like GPUs. To bridge this gap, we introduce a fully GPU-native trajectory optimization framework that combines sequential convex programming with a consensus-based alternating direction method of multipliers. By applying a temporal splitting strategy, our algorithm decouples the optimization horizon into independent, per-node subproblems that execute massively in parallel. The entire process runs fully on the GPU, eliminating costly memory transfers and large-scale sparse factorizations. This architecture naturally scales to multi-trajectory optimization. We validate the solver on a quadrotor agile flight task and a Mars powered descent problem using an on-board edge computing platform. Benchmarks reveal a sustained 4x throughput speedup and a 51% reduction in energy consumption over a heavily optimized 12-core CPU baseline. Crucially, the framework saturates the hardware, maintaining over 96% active GPU utilization to achieve planning rates exceeding 100 Hz. Furthermore, we demonstrate the solver's extensibility to robust Model Predictive Control by jointly optimizing dynamically coupled scenarios under stochastic disturbances, enabling scalable and safe autonomy.

</details>


### [182] [FutureVLA: Joint Visuomotor Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10712)
*Xiaoxu Xu,Hao Li,Jinhui Ye,Yilun Chen,Jia Zeng,Xinyi Chen,Linning Xu,Dahua Lin,Weixin Li,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureVLA提出了一种联合视觉运动预测架构，通过解耦视觉和运动信息并联合编码广义物理先验，解决了现有VLA模型在预测未来时视觉主导、时间连续性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体需要预测性远见，但现有视觉-语言-动作模型难以有效建模视觉感知与环境几何之间的紧密耦合关系。显式方法关注任务无关的视觉细节，隐式方法依赖稀疏帧对破坏了时间连续性，且都过度依赖视觉重建，导致静态场景上下文与动态动作意图纠缠。

Method: 提出FutureVLA框架，包含联合视觉运动预测架构：1) 预训练阶段利用异构操作数据集，引入联合视觉运动门控机制，结构上分离视觉状态保持与时间动作建模；2) 后训练阶段采用潜在嵌入对齐策略，使下游VLA模型能够内化这些时间先验而不改变推理架构。

Result: 大量实验表明，FutureVLA能够持续改进各种VLA框架的性能，通过解耦视觉和运动信息并联合编码物理先验，实现了更好的时间连续性和视觉条件监督解耦。

Conclusion: 有效的联合视觉运动预测建模需要时间连续性和视觉条件监督解耦。FutureVLA通过其联合视觉运动预测架构成功实现了这一目标，为智能体提供了更好的预测性远见能力。

Abstract: Predictive foresight is important to intelligent embodied agents. Since the motor execution of a robot is intrinsically constrained by its visual perception of environmental geometry, effectively anticipating the future requires capturing this tightly coupled visuomotor interplay. While recent vision-language-action models attempt to incorporate future guidance, they struggle with this joint modeling. Existing explicit methods divert capacity to task-irrelevant visual details, whereas implicit methods relying on sparse frame pairs disrupt temporal continuity. By heavily relying on visual reconstruction, these methods become visually dominated, entangling static scene context with dynamic action intent. We argue that effective joint visuomotor predictive modeling requires both temporal continuity and visually-conditioned supervision decoupling. To this end, we propose FutureVLA, featuring a novel Joint Visuomotor Predictive Architecture. FutureVLA is designed to extract joint visuomotor embeddings by first decoupling visual and motor information, and then jointly encoding generalized physical priors. Specifically, in the pretraining stage, we leverage heterogeneous manipulation datasets and introduce a Joint Visuomotor Gating mechanism to structurally separate visual state preservation from temporal action modeling. It allows the motor stream to focus on continuous physical dynamics while explicitly querying visual tokens for environmental constraints, yielding highly generalizable joint visuomotor embeddings. Subsequently, in the post-training stage, we employ a latent embeddings alignment strategy, enabling diverse downstream VLA models to internalize these temporal priors without modifying their inference architectures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FutureVLA consistently improves VLA frameworks.

</details>


### [183] [MAVEN: A Meta-Reinforcement Learning Framework for Varying-Dynamics Expertise in Agile Quadrotor Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10714)
*Jin Zhou,Dongcheng Cao,Xian Wang,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAVEN：一种元强化学习框架，使单个策略能够在广泛的四旋翼动力学变化中实现鲁棒的端到端导航，通过预测性上下文编码器从交互历史中推断系统动力学，并在质量变化高达66.7%和单旋翼推力损失达70%的情况下实现零样本仿真到实物的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习训练的策略通常无法在显著动态变化中泛化，缺乏适应性。四旋翼导航需要应对质量变化和单旋翼推力损失等动态变化，因此需要开发能够适应广泛动力学变化的鲁棒导航策略。

Method: 提出MAVEN元强化学习框架，包含新颖的预测性上下文编码器，从交互历史中学习推断系统动力学的潜在表示。使用GPU向量化仿真器在数千个并行环境中分布任务，克服元强化学习训练时间长的挑战，在不到一小时内收敛。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证MAVEN实现了优越的适应性和敏捷性。策略成功执行零样本仿真到实物迁移，在质量变化高达66.7%和单旋翼推力损失达70%的情况下仍能执行高速机动，展示了鲁棒的在线适应能力。

Conclusion: MAVEN框架通过元强化学习和预测性上下文编码器，使单个策略能够在广泛的四旋翼动力学变化中实现鲁棒的端到端导航，解决了标准强化学习策略泛化能力不足的问题，为动态变化环境中的敏捷导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for achieving online agile navigation with quadrotors. Despite this success, policies trained via standard RL typically fail to generalize across significant dynamic variations, exhibiting a critical lack of adaptability. This work introduces MAVEN, a meta-RL framework that enables a single policy to achieve robust end-to-end navigation across a wide range of quadrotor dynamics. Our approach features a novel predictive context encoder, which learns to infer a latent representation of the system dynamics from interaction history. We demonstrate our method in agile waypoint traversal tasks under two challenging scenarios: large variations in quadrotor mass and severe single-rotor thrust loss. We leverage a GPU-vectorized simulator to distribute tasks across thousands of parallel environments, overcoming the long training times of meta-RL to converge in less than an hour. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world, we validate that MAVEN achieves superior adaptation and agility. The policy successfully executes zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, demonstrating robust online adaptation by performing high-speed maneuvers despite mass variations of up to 66.7% and single-rotor thrust losses as severe as 70%.

</details>


### [184] [ASTER: Attitude-aware Suspended-payload Quadrotor Traversal via Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10715)
*Dongcheng Cao,Jin Zhou,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASTER：首个实现四旋翼缆索悬挂系统自主倒飞（inverted flight）的强化学习框架，通过混合动力学状态播种（HDSS）解决姿态约束下的极端奖励稀疏问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼缆索悬挂系统的敏捷机动受到其非光滑混合动力学的严重阻碍。虽然无模型强化学习避免了复杂模型的显式微分，但在严格姿态要求下实现姿态约束或倒飞仍然是一个开放挑战，因为极端奖励稀疏性使得策略难以学习

Method: 提出ASTER框架，核心是混合动力学状态播种（HDSS）初始化策略。该方法通过物理一致的逆运动学将目标配置反向传播到缆索张紧和松弛两个阶段，使策略能够发现标准探索无法达到的激进机动动作

Result: 在大量仿真和真实世界实验中展示了卓越的敏捷性、精确的姿态对齐能力，以及跨复杂轨迹的鲁棒零样本仿真到现实迁移。实现了首个自主倒飞的四旋翼缆索悬挂系统

Conclusion: ASTER框架通过HDSS策略成功解决了四旋翼缆索悬挂系统在姿态约束下的极端奖励稀疏问题，实现了首个自主倒飞，为混合动力学系统的敏捷控制提供了新思路

Abstract: Agile maneuvering of the quadrotor cable-suspended system is significantly hindered by its non-smooth hybrid dynamics. While model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) circumvents explicit differentiation of complex models, achieving attitude-constrained or inverted flight remains an open challenge due to the extreme reward sparsity under strict orientation requirements. This paper presents ASTER, a robust RL framework that achieves, to our knowledge, the first successful autonomous inverted flight for the cable-suspended system. We propose hybrid-dynamics-informed state seeding (HDSS), an initialization strategy that back-propagates target configurations through physics-consistent kinematic inversions across both taut and slack cable phases. HDSS enables the policy to discover aggressive maneuvers that are unreachable via standard exploration. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate remarkable agility, precise attitude alignment, and robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across complex trajectories.

</details>


### [185] [Semantic Landmark Particle Filter for Robot Localisation in Vineyards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10847)
*Rajitha de Silva,Jonathan Cox,James R. Heselden,Marija Popović,Cesar Cadena,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLPF方法，通过整合树干和杆状地标检测与2D LiDAR，解决葡萄园中行级感知混淆问题，提高定位精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄园定位面临行级感知混淆挑战：平行的作物行产生几乎相同的LiDAR观测，导致仅依赖几何或视觉的SLAM系统容易收敛到错误的走廊，特别是在地头转弯时

Method: 提出语义地标粒子滤波器（SLPF），将树干和杆状地标检测与2D LiDAR整合到概率定位框架中。检测到的树干被转换为语义墙，形成结构化的行边界嵌入测量模型，以更好区分相邻行。同时整合GNSS作为轻量级先验，在语义观测稀疏时稳定定位

Result: 在10行葡萄园的现场实验中，相比仅几何方法（AMCL）、基于视觉方法（RTAB-Map）和GNSS基线均有显著改进。相比AMCL，SLPF在两个遍历方向上分别减少绝对姿态误差22%和65%；相比噪声GNSS基线，APE分别减少65%和61%。行正确率从0.67提高到0.73，平均横向误差从1.40米减少到1.26米

Conclusion: 将行级结构语义嵌入测量模型中，能够在高度重复的户外农业环境中实现鲁棒定位，有效解决葡萄园中的感知混淆问题

Abstract: Reliable localisation in vineyards is hindered by row-level perceptual aliasing: parallel crop rows produce nearly identical LiDAR observations, causing geometry-only and vision-based SLAM systems to converge towards incorrect corridors, particularly during headland transitions. We present a Semantic Landmark Particle Filter (SLPF) that integrates trunk and pole landmark detections with 2D LiDAR within a probabilistic localisation framework. Detected trunks are converted into semantic walls, forming structural row boundaries embedded in the measurement model to improve discrimination between adjacent rows. GNSS is incorporated as a lightweight prior that stabilises localisation when semantic observations are sparse.
  Field experiments in a 10-row vineyard demonstrate consistent improvements over geometry-only (AMCL), vision-based (RTAB-Map), and GNSS baselines. Compared to AMCL, SLPF reduces Absolute Pose Error by 22% and 65% across two traversal directions; relative to a NoisyGNSS baseline, APE decreases by 65% and 61%. Row correctness improves from 0.67 to 0.73, while mean cross-track error decreases from 1.40 m to 1.26 m. These results show that embedding row-level structural semantics within the measurement model enables robust localisation in highly repetitive outdoor agricultural environments.

</details>


### [186] [GRACE: A Unified 2D Multi-Robot Path Planning Simulator & Benchmark for Grid, Roadmap, And Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10858)
*Chuanlong Zang,Anna Mannucci,Isabelle Barz,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRACE是一个统一的多智能体路径规划仿真平台，支持网格、路网和连续空间三种抽象级别，提供可复现的比较基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体路径规划工具要么在简化假设下扩展（网格、同质智能体），要么提供高保真度但缺乏可比性。需要透明、可复现的平台来比较不同建模选择。

Method: 开发GRACE统一2D仿真+基准平台，通过显式、可复现的操作符在多个抽象级别（网格、路网、连续空间）实例化相同任务，并使用共同评估协议。

Result: 在公共地图和代表性规划器上的实证结果实现了共享实例集上的可比比较。量化了表示-保真度权衡：连续空间规划器保真度高但速度慢，网格/路网规划器扩展性更好。

Conclusion: GRACE通过整合表示、执行和评估，使跨表示研究更具可比性，为推进多机器人规划研究及其实际应用提供了工具。

Abstract: Advancing Multi-Agent Pathfinding (MAPF) and Multi-Robot Motion Planning (MRMP) requires platforms that enable transparent, reproducible comparisons across modeling choices. Existing tools either scale under simplifying assumptions (grids, homogeneous agents) or offer higher fidelity with less comparable instrumentation. We present GRACE, a unified 2D simulator+benchmark that instantiates the same task at multiple abstraction levels (grid, roadmap, continuous) via explicit, reproducible operators and a common evaluation protocol. Our empirical results on public maps and representative planners enable commensurate comparisons on a shared instance set. Furthermore, we quantify the expected representation-fidelity trade-offs (MRMP solves instances at higher fidelity but lower speed, while grid/roadmap planners scale farther). By consolidating representation, execution, and evaluation, GRACE thereby aims to make cross-representation studies more comparable and provides a means to advance multi-robot planning research and its translation to practice.

</details>


### [187] [FG-CLTP: Fine-Grained Contrastive Language Tactile Pretraining for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10871)
*Wenxuan Ma,Chaofan Zhang,Yinghao Cai,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FG-CLTP：一种细粒度对比语言触觉预训练框架，通过3D点云-语言对数据集和离散化数值标记化机制，将定量接触状态（力、几何、方向）融入多模态特征空间，显著提升触觉感知精度并减少仿真-现实差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉表示主要依赖定性描述符（如纹理），忽略了力大小、接触几何和主轴方向等定量接触状态，而这些对于细粒度机器人操作至关重要。需要一种能够捕捉多维接触状态的细粒度触觉表示方法。

Method: 提出FG-CLTP框架：1）构建包含10万+触觉3D点云-语言对的数据集，从传感器视角明确捕捉多维接触状态；2）实现离散化数值标记化机制，实现定量-语义对齐，将显式物理度量注入多模态特征空间；3）基于此表示开发3D触觉-语言-动作（3D-TLA）架构，采用流匹配策略进行多模态推理和控制。

Result: FG-CLTP模型达到95.9%的分类准确率，回归误差（MAE）相比最先进方法降低52.6%。3D点云表示建立了传感器无关的基础，仿真-现实差距仅为3.5%。在接触丰富的操作任务中，该框架显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: FG-CLTP框架通过将定量接触状态融入触觉表示，为触觉-语言-动作模型提供了鲁棒且可泛化的基础，显著提升了细粒度机器人操作的感知和控制能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in integrating tactile sensing into vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated transformative potential for robotic perception. However, existing tactile representations predominantly rely on qualitative descriptors (e.g., texture), neglecting quantitative contact states such as force magnitude, contact geometry, and principal axis orientation, which are indispensable for fine-grained manipulation. To bridge this gap, we propose FG-CLTP, a fine-grained contrastive language tactile pretraining framework. We first introduce a novel dataset comprising over 100k tactile 3D point cloud-language pairs that explicitly capture multidimensional contact states from the sensor's perspective. We then implement a discretized numerical tokenization mechanism to achieve quantitative-semantic alignment, effectively injecting explicit physical metrics into the multimodal feature space. The proposed FG-CLTP model yields a 95.9% classification accuracy and reduces the regression error (MAE) by 52.6% compared to state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, the integration of 3D point cloud representations establishes a sensor-agnostic foundation with a minimal sim-to-real gap of 3.5%. Building upon this fine-grained representation, we develop a 3D tactile-language-action (3D-TLA) architecture driven by a flow matching policy to enable multimodal reasoning and control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms strong baselines in contact-rich manipulation tasks, providing a robust and generalizable foundation for tactile-language-action models.

</details>


### [188] [A gripper for flap separation and opening of sealed bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10890)
*Sergi Foix,Jaume Oriol,Carme Torras,Júlia Borràs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种新型夹持器，专门用于分离和抓取薄柔性层（如医用无菌袋封口），通过主动齿状滚轮指尖和柔性手指设计，实现了可靠的封口分离和打开操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临床环境中分离薄柔性层（如无菌袋封口）的挑战性操作问题。护士需要手动打开大量无菌袋（每班次最多240个），这是一项重复性高、体力消耗大且与肌肉骨骼损伤相关的任务，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 设计了一种新型夹持器，结合主动齿状滚轮指尖和柔性手指，利用环境约束来稳健抓取薄柔性封口。通过两个夹持器同时抓取两个封口，系统能够承受打开密封所需的力量。

Result: 实验表明，该夹持器能够可靠地抓取和分离密封袋封口以及其他医院环境中的薄层材料。影响性能的最敏感变量是施加的法向力。当两个夹持器同时抓取两个封口时，系统能够稳健地承受打开密封所需的力量。

Conclusion: 这是首批展示机器人辅助自动化这一重复性、低价值但关键的医院任务的成果之一。该设计为解决薄柔性层分离这一普遍但具有挑战性的操作问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Separating thin, flexible layers that must be individually grasped is a common but challenging manipulation primitive for most off-the-shelf grippers. A prominent example arises in clinical settings: the opening of sterile flat pouches for the preparation of the operating room, where the first step is to separate and grasp the flaps. We present a novel gripper design and opening strategy that enables reliable flap separation and robust seal opening. This capability addresses a high-volume repetitive hospital procedure in which nurses manually open up to 240 bags per shift, a physically demanding task linked to musculoskeletal injuries. Our design combines an active dented-roller fingertip with compliant fingers that exploit environmental constraints to robustly grasp thin flexible flaps.
